Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. Stimuli rotate\n    13\t// through STIM_BLOCKS precomputed blocks so the opcode never sees a constant.\n    14\t//\n    15\t// Dump: a fresh single-copy VM is run for --blocks blocks; the opcode's two\n    16\t// output buffers (out, out+1) are written as raw float32, L block then R\n    17\t// block. This is the A/B oracle for the equality gate \u2014 the stimuli are the\n    18\t// same ones the timing path uses (one source of truth).\n    19\t\n    20\t#include \"cedar/vm/vm.hpp\"\n    21\t#include \"cedar/vm/instruction.hpp\"\n    22\t#include \"cedar/dsp/simd.hpp\"\n    23\t\n    24\t#include <algorithm>\n    25\t#include <array>\n    26\t#include <chrono>\n    27\t#include <cmath>\n    28\t#include <cstdint>\n    29\t#include <cstdio>\n    30\t#include <cstring>\n    31\t#include <fstream>\n    32\t#include <functional>\n    33\t#include <memory>\n    34\t#include <random>\n    35\t#include <string>\n    36\t#include <string_view>\n    37\t#include <vector>\n    38\t\n    39\tusing namespace cedar;\n    40\t\n    41\tnamespace {\n    42\t\n    43\tconstexpr std::size_t INSTANCES = 64;\n    44\tconstexpr std::size_t STIM_BLOCKS = 256;\n    45\tconstexpr std::uint16_t IN_BASE = 0;    // stimulus buffers 0..4\n    46\tconstexpr std::uint16_t OUT_BASE = 8;   // outputs at 8 + 2k (stereo pairs)\n    47\tconstexpr float SR = 48000.0f;\n    48\tconstexpr std::uint16_t NONE = 0xFFFF;\n    49\t\n    50\t// Stimulus for one input slot at absolute sample index n (0..STIM_BLOCKS*BLOCK_SIZE).\n    51\tusing Stim = std::function<float(std::size_t n)>;\n    52\t\n    53\t// --seed N (N != 0) switches every stimulus to a randomised \"hidden\" variant\n    54\t// (PRD \u00a76 / research P5): the full verify picks a fresh seed per call and\n    55\t// dumps baseline and candidate with it, so a kernel cannot be tuned to the\n    56\t// fixed stimuli the quick check and the timing path use.\n    57\tstd::uint32_t g_seed = 0;\n    58\tstd::mt19937 g_hidden_rng;  // draws per-stimulus variations in hidden mode\n    59\t\n    60\tfloat frac(std::size_t n, std::size_t period) {\n    61\t    return static_cast<float>(n % period) / static_cast<float>(period);\n    62\t}\n    63\t\n    64\t// Audio-rate signal input: seeded noise \u2192 log sine sweep \u2192 impulse train \u2192\n    65\t// ramp, a quarter of the stimulus window each (PRD \u00a74.3 fixed stimuli).\n    66\tstruct AudioStim {\n    67\t    float gain;\n    68\t    std::vector<float> noise;\n    69\t    explicit AudioStim(float g) : gain(g), noise(STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE) {\n    70\t        std::mt19937 rng(g_seed ? g_seed : 0xCEDA5u);\n    71\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> d(-1.0f, 1.0f);\n    72\t        for (auto& x : noise) x = d(rng);\n    73\t        if (g_seed) make_hidden(rng);\n    74\t    }\n    75\t    // Hidden mode: 8 segments, each a random choice of noise at a random\n    76\t    // gain (from near-denormal to 4x the nominal level), sparse random\n    77\t    // impulses, DC steps, or silence. Every seed is guaranteed one\n    78\t    // near-denormal noise segment (1e-35..1e-25) and one hot one (2-4x),\n    79\t    // at random positions, so that coverage never depends on luck.\n    80\t    void make_hidden(std::mt19937& rng) {\n    81\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n    82\t        constexpr std::size_t seg = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE / 8;\n    83\t        const auto tiny_seg = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 8.0f) % 8;\n    84\t        const auto hot_seg = (tiny_seg + 1 + static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 7.0f) % 7) % 8;\n    85\t        for (std::size_t s = 0; s < 8; ++s) {\n    86\t            int kind = static_cast<int>(u(rng) * 4.0f);\n    87\t            float level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -30.0f * u(rng) * u(rng)) * (1.0f + 3.0f * u(rng));\n    88\t            if (s == tiny_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -25.0f - 10.0f * u(rng)); }\n    89\t            if (s == hot_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * (2.0f + 2.0f * u(rng)); }\n    90\t            float dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f);\n    91\t            for (std::size_t k = s * seg; k < (s + 1) * seg; ++k) {\n    92\t                switch (kind) {\n    93\t                    case 0: noise[k] *= level; break;\n    94\t                    case 1: noise[k] = u(rng) < 0.002f ? level * (u(rng) < 0.5f ? -1.0f : 1.0f) : 0.0f; break;\n    95\t                    case 2: noise[k] = (k % 4096 == 0) ? (dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f)) : dc; break;\n    96\t                    default: noise[k] = 0.0f; break;\n    97\t                }\n    98\t            }\n    99\t        }\n   100\t    }\n   101\t    float operator()(std::size_t n) const {\n   102\t        if (g_seed) return noise[n];\n   103\t        constexpr std::size_t total = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE;\n   104\t        constexpr std::size_t quarter = total / 4;\n   105\t        std::size_t seg = n / quarter, k = n % quarter;\n   106\t        float t = static_cast<float>(k) / static_cast<float>(quarter);\n   107\t        switch (seg) {\n   108\t            case 0: return gain * noise[n];\n   109\t            case 1: {\n   110\t                // 20 Hz \u2192 20 kHz exponential sweep, phase-integrated\n   111\t                double f0 = 20.0, f1 = 20000.0, T = double(quarter) / double(SR);\n   112\t                double tt = double(k) / double(SR);\n   113\t                double kk = std::log(f1 / f0) / T;\n   114\t                double ph = 2.0 * 3.14159265358979 * f0 * (std::exp(kk * tt) - 1.0) / kk;\n   115\t                return gain * static_cast<float>(std::sin(ph));\n   116\t            }\n   117\t            case 2: return (k % 997 == 0) ? gain : 0.0f;\n   118\t            default: return gain * (2.0f * t - 1.0f);\n   119\t        }\n   120\t    }\n   121\t};\n   122\t\n   123\tStim ramp(float lo, float hi, std::size_t period) {\n   124\t    if (g_seed) {  // hidden: same range, random rate and phase\n   125\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n   126\t        auto p = static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<float>(period) * (0.05f + 3.0f * u(g_hidden_rng))) + 1;\n   127\t        auto off = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(g_hidden_rng) * static_cast<float>(p));\n   128\t        return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n + off, p); };\n   129\t    }\n   130\t    return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n, period); };\n   131\t}\n   132\tStim constant(float v) { return [=](std::size_t) { return v; }; }\n   133\t\n   134\tstruct Case {\n   135\t    const char* name;\n   136\t    Opcode op;\n   137\t    std::vector<Stim> inputs; i < argc; ++i) {\n   249\t        std::string_view a = argv[i];\n   250\t        auto next = [&]() -> const char* { return i + 1 < argc ? argv[++i] : nullptr; };\n   251\t        const char* v = nullptr;\n   252\t        if (a == \"--list\") list = true;\n   253\t        else if (a == \"--json\") json = true;\n   254\t        else if (a == \"--opcode\" && (v = next())) opcode = v;\n   255\t        else if (a == \"--dump\" && (v = next())) dump = v;\n   256\t        else if (a == \"--reps\" && (v = next())) reps = std::stoul(v);\n   257\t        else if (a == \"--warmup\" && (v = next())) warmup = std::stoul(v);\n   258\t        else if (a == \"--blocks\" && (v = next())) blocks = std::stoul(v);\n   259\t        else if (a == \"--scalar\") simd::force(simd::Isa::Scalar);\n   260\t        else if (a == \"--seed\" && (v = next())) g_seed = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::stoul(v));\n   261\t        else return usage();\n   262\t    }\n   263\t\n   264\t    g_hidden_rng.seed(g_seed);\n   265\t    auto cases = make_cases();\n   266\t    if (list) {\n   267\t        for (const auto& c : cases) std::printf(\"%s\\n\", c.name);\n   268\t        return 0;\n   269\t    }\n   270\t    auto it = std::find_if(cases.begin(), cases.end(),\n   271\t                           [&](const Case& c) { return opcode == c.name; });\n   272\t    if (it == cases.end()) {\n   273\t        std::fprintf(stderr, \"unknown opcode '%s' (see --list)\\n\", opcode.c_str());\n   274\t        return 2;\n   275\t    }\n   276\t\n   277\t    if (!dump.empty()) {\n   278\t        Rig rig(*it, 1);\n   279\t        std::ofstream f(dump, std::ios::binary);\n   280\t        for (std::size_t b = 0; b < warmup; ++b) rig.block(b);\n   292\t    std::vector<double> ns(reps);\n   293\t    // One rep = REP_BLOCKS blocks spread evenly over the stimulus window, so\n   294\t    // every rep sees every stimulus segment (tanh cost is input-dependent;\n   295\t    // per-block timing would make p10/p90 measure the stimulus, not noise).\n   296\t    // Stimulus copies stay outside the timed region.\n   297\t    // An empty-program VM is timed interleaved with the real one and\n   298\t    // subtracted: process_block has ~450 ns of fixed per-block overhead,\n   299\t    // which otherwise swamps cheap opcodes like op_mul.\n   300\t    constexpr std::size_t REP_BLOCKS = 32;\n   301\t    Rig empty(*it, 0);\n   302\t    using clk = std::chrono::steady_clock;\n   303\t    auto dt = [](clk::time_point a, clk::time_point b) {\n   304\t        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::nano>(b - a).count();\n   305\t    };\n   306\t    for (std::size_t r = 0; r < reps; ++r) {\n   307\t        double t = 0.0;\n   308\t        for (std::size_t j = 0; j < REP_BLOCKS; ++j) {\n   309\t            rig.load(r + j * (STIM_BLOCKS / REP_BLOCKS));\n   310\t            auto t0 = clk::now();\n   311\t            empty.run();\n   312\t            auto t1 = clk::now();\n   313\t            rig.run();\n   314\t            auto t2 = clk::now();\n   315\t            t += dt(t1, t2) - dt(t0, t1);\n   316\t        }\n   317\t        ns[r] = t / (REP_BLOCKS * INSTANCES);\n   318\t    }\n   319\t    std::sort(ns.begin(), ns.end());\n   320\t    auto pct = [&](double p) { return ns[static_cast<std::size_t>(p * double(reps - 1))]; };\n   321\t    double med = pct(0.5), p10 = pct(0.1), p90 = pct(0.9);\n   322\t\n   323\t    std::string cpu = read_first_line(\"/proc/cpuinfo\", \"model name\");\n   324\t    std::string gov = read_first_line(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/scaling_governor\");\n   325\t    const char* isa = simd::name(simd::active());\n   326\t\n   327\t    if (json) {\n   328\t        std::printf(\"{\\\"opcode\\\":\\\"%s\\\",\\\"ns_per_block_median\\\":%.2f,\\\"ns_p10\\\":%.2f,\"\n   329\t                    \"\\\"ns_p90\\\":%.2f,\\\"reps\\\":%zu,\\\"isa\\\":\\\"%s\\\",\\\"cpu\\\":\\\"%s\\\",\"\n   330\t                    \"\\\"governor\\\":\\\"%s\\\"}\\n\",\n   331\t                    it->name, med, p10, p90, reps, isa, json_escape(cpu).c_str(),\n   332\t                    json_escape(gov).c_str());\n   333\t    } else {\n   334\t        std::printf(\"%-20s median %8.2f ns/block  p10 %8.2f  p90 %8.2f  (%s, %s)\\n\",\n   335\t                    it->name, med, p10, p90, isa, gov.c_str());\n   336\t    }\n   337\t    if (gov != \"performance\")\n   338\t        std::fprintf(stderr, \"warning: governor is '%s', not 'performance' \u2014 results unstable\\n\",\n   339\t                     gov.c_str());\n   340\t    return 0;\n   341\t}"

This enforces \"the scalar body is never deleted\" mechanically.\n   548\t4. **Noise band = run-to-run spread of baseline medians** over 5 interleaved\n   549\t   rounds. The within-run p10/p90 spread is still reported, but it measures\n   550\t   interrupt tails (30 % on `op_mul`) rather than median noise (0.5 %). The bench\n   551\t   also subtracts an interleaved empty-program VM (~450 ns of fixed\n   552\t   `process_block` overhead that hid most of `op_mul`'s cost) and times each\n   553\t   rep over 32 blocks spread across all four stimulus segments (`tanh`'s cost\n   554\t   depends on the input).\n   555\t5. **No per-ISA kernel TUs.** Kernels carry `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`\n   556\t   (`CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2`) in the opcode headers, so no CMake flags change and the\n   557\t   allowlist stays header-only. `CEDAR_SIMD` defaults **OFF**; the `simd`\n   558\t   preset (Release + tests) is the gate build, and `wasm-simd` adds\n   559\t   `-msimd128`."

The 1-sample drop at every `.gate` onset means ADSR releases-and-reattacks between adjacent notes by default; overlap durations (`pat.dur(2)`) to suppress the drop and get legato.\nCLAUDE.md:102: - `poly(input, instrument, voices)` allocates a new runtime voice per incoming note (instrument is 3-arg `(freq, gate, vel)`, may return stereo).\nCLAUDE.md:103: - `unison(freq, gate, vel, instrument, voices, detune, width, phase)` is a userspace stdlib fn that statically fans a single note into N detuned, stereo-panned, phase-spread voices (instrument is 4-arg `(freq, gate, vel, ext)` with `ext.{idx, count, detune_st, pan, phase}`).
